# Holo Messenger inventory

Merged from the Element Web inventory (676 rows, W- IDs) and the Element X / Element Call inventory (445 rows, X- IDs), each with its parity pass. Read 2026-11-18.
Parity, phase and acceptance checks are recommendations for Ilya to overrule. Nothing here is copied from Element source; descriptions are in our own words.
Engine standing follows `matrix-to-uor-comms-map.md` as read on 2026-11-18, a snapshot. uor-comms is canonical for what the engine supplies and when: its `docs/DESIGN.md` and `docs/STATUS.md` override the "uor-comms need" and "engine standing" columns here. Phases in this file are the client's release phases; they do not name uor-comms's own phases.

## How to read

- HM ID: stable; assigned per kind, in domain order. Sources lists the W- and X- rows the item came from.
- Element source: `web:` paths are relative to `apps/web/src` in element-hq/element-web unless they start with `apps/`, `packages/` or `docs/`; `x:` paths are as the mobile inventory wrote them (`ios:` element-x-ios, `and:` element-x-android, plus element-call and element-meta paths).
- uor-comms need: the one mapping-table concept that gates the item. Where merged rows named different needs, the one with the weakest standing is shown.
- Engine standing: proved rule (a proved decision whose inputs a host must supply) / different shape (Prism ID has it in code, not wired into uor-comms) / missing / undecided / not needed (client-only, or server-only and dropped).
- Parity: keep / adapt / drop. Phase: 1, 2, 3, or - for drop.

## Open for Ilya

Verifying another person was split on 2026-11-19 (Ilya, taking the recommended path). Phase 1 verifies your own devices only, which Prism ID supplies through its device link; verifying another person, the verified badge on contacts and messages, and the per-user verification card are Phase 2, since uor-comms has not designed confirming that a contact's Prism ID is theirs. Rows changed: HM-CAP-109 and HM-SUR-119 to Phase 2; HM-FEA-122, HM-SUR-129, HM-SUR-130 and HM-SUR-132 scoped to own devices; HM-SUR-005, HM-FEA-047 and HM-SUR-138 no longer wait on verifying another person.

Three parity calls were changed from keep to adapt on 2026-11-19, to follow WhatsApp's placement: HM-FEA-074 (times always shown, only the clock format offered), HM-SUR-074 (readers listed in Message info, not stacked under the message) and HM-SUR-097 (typing named under the chat name, not in a row over the composer). Each acceptance check was rewritten to match. They stand until Ilya overrules them.

6 pairs sit on the two phase conflicts in the brief: device verification is Phase 1 but multi-device is Phase 2, and restoring identity is Phase 1 but recovery is Phase 2. They are not merged; each side keeps its own item and call until the phases are settled.
